# Third-Party Licenses

이 소프트웨어에 들어가는 제3자 크레이트와 자료의 라이선스 고지를 모은 파일입니다.
This file collects the license notices for the third-party crates and materials built into this software.

- Generated by `vanilla-engine-licenses` from `cargo metadata` and the license files in each crate package. Only the bundled materials section between the markers is written by hand; the rest is regenerated.
- Targets: `x86_64-pc-windows-msvc`, `aarch64-pc-windows-msvc`, `x86_64-apple-darwin`, `aarch64-apple-darwin`, `x86_64-unknown-linux-gnu`, `aarch64-unknown-linux-gnu`, `wasm32-unknown-unknown`. Normal and build dependencies are included; dev-dependencies are not.
- Where a crate offers a choice of licenses, the license used is the one shown after "used under".
- Crates: 351 (346 with notices below, 5 under licenses that require no notice). License texts: 183.

## Bundled Materials

<!-- vanilla-engine-licenses: bundled materials begin -->
The engine crate (vanilla-engine) contains the following code, tables and data from other projects. They are not crates, so their notices are kept here by hand. Each entry names the engine files and the original files.

### HRTF data (IRCAM LISTEN)

- `src/platform/audio/hrtf/resources/Composite.flac`, the HRTF database Chromium ships, is built into desktop builds. Its origin and terms are in `src/platform/audio/hrtf/resources/README.md`.
- IRCAM requests, as a courtesy, a written acknowledgment when the materials are used in commercial products: IRCAM - 1, place Stravinsky 75004 PARIS FRANCE.

### fdlibm

- `src/platform/audio/hrtf/fdlibm.rs` (log, log10, pow, desktop builds) is ported from Chromium `third_party/fdlibm/ieee754.cc`, which is adapted from fdlibm. The Chromium file notes that the original source "has been modified significantly by Google Inc." (Copyright 2016 the V8 project authors, Copyright 2020 The Chromium Authors, under the Chromium license below).

### LLVM libc

- `src/base/trigonometry.rs` (`atan2` and its helpers `ordered_two_sum`, `ordered_add`, `fused_two_product`, `fused_divide`, `atan_small`): the order of operations of the double precision `atan2` of LLVM libc (`src/__support/math/atan2.h`, `atan_utils.h`, `FPUtil/double_double.h`), which Chrome's V8 calls for `Math.atan2`, so the engine returns the same bits as Chrome. Part of the LLVM Project, under the Apache License v2.0 with LLVM Exceptions (Apache 2.0 full text in License Texts below, or https://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0).


### Chromium and Blink

- Web Audio (desktop mixer, `src/platform/audio/`: audioparam.rs, panner.rs, pannermath.rs, oscillator.rs, mixer.rs, decode/resample.rs, decode/opus.rs, decode/trim.rs, hrtf/): third_party/blink/renderer/modules/webaudio/ (audio_param_handler.cc, panner_handler.cc, audio_listener_handler.cc, oscillator_handler.cc, periodic_wave_handler.cc, audio_scheduled_source_handler.cc, audio_buffer_source_handler.cc, cpu/arm/oscillator_kernel_neon.cc, cpu/x86/oscillator_kernel_sse2.cc), media/base/sinc_resampler.cc, ui/gfx/geometry/vector3d_f.cc, and the platform audio files listed below. Decoding (`src/platform/audio/decode/`): media/filters/opus_audio_decoder.cc and media/formats/common/opus_constants.h (opus.rs), media/filters/audio_file_reader.cc and media/base/audio_discard_helper.cc (trim.rs).
- Text editing (`src/core/textinput.rs`): third_party/blink/renderer/core/editing/state_machines/backspace_state_machine.cc and position_units_word.cc.
- Canvas 2D paths (`src/core/canvas2d.rs`): third_party/blink/renderer/modules/canvas/canvas2d/canvas_path.cc (with Skia, below).
- Text (`src/core/textshaping.rs`): tables of third_party/blink/renderer/platform/text/character_property_data.h.
- UI strings (`src/core/browseruitext.json`, desktop builds): translations from Chromium's generated_resources, ui_strings and components_strings .xtb files.
- Desktop platform (`src/platform/desktop/`): the font family tables of Chromium's locale_settings resources and platform behavior (linuxplatform.rs: ui/base/pointer/pointer_device_linux.cc).

### ICU4C and CLDR (Unicode License V3)

- `src/core/component/text.rs` (desktop builds): the dictionary segmentation of ICU4C `CjkBreakEngine::divideUpDictionaryRange` (dictbe.cpp) with its katakana cost values.
- `src/base/format.rs`: number format data from CLDR (read through the Intl API of Node.js, ICU 78.3, CLDR 48). CLDR is "Copyright © 2001–2026 Unicode, Inc." and its data is released under the same Unicode License V3.

### ICT FaceKit and the Infinite head scan (example assets read from vanilla.js, not in the crate)

- `examples/oldface.rs` reads `character_lod1.glb` and its textures from the vanilla.js repository (`examples/oldface/assets`). The face topology, UVs, eyes, teeth, eyelashes, eye occlusion and expression shapes come from ICT FaceKit (FaceXModel), https://github.com/USC-ICT/ICT-FaceKit (MIT, below).
- The head shape and the skin textures (`head_albedo.jpg`, `head_normal.jpg`, `skin_detail.jpg`, `head_roughness.jpg`, `head_specular.jpg`) come from "Infinite, 3D Head Scan" by Lee Perry-Smith (Infinite-Realities, I-R Entertainment Ltd.), based on a work at www.triplegangers.com, CC BY 3.0, https://creativecommons.org/licenses/by/3.0/ . Changed: the ICT head is reshaped to the scan and the colour and displacement are re-baked onto the ICT UVs, stubble and brows removed, beard shadow lifted, tone shifted. The example shows this credit on screen.
- The wrinkle, eye, teeth and hair textures and the hair cards are baked procedurally for vanilla.js and are under its MIT License.

````text

Please remember: Do what you want with the files, but always mention where you got them from...
````

### Public domain and CC0 code

No notice is required for these. They are listed so that the origin of the code is clear.

- `src/effect/screeneffect.rs`, `src/effect/shadersprite.rs`, `src/graphics3d/toon/toonvfx.rs` (hash21, hashPoint): the fmix32 finalizer of MurmurHash3 by Austin Appleby (https://github.com/aappleby/smhasher/blob/master/src/MurmurHash3.cpp): "MurmurHash3 was written by Austin Appleby, and is placed in the public domain. The author hereby disclaims copyright to this source code."
- `src/effect/screeneffect.rs` (tonemap), `src/graphics3d/toon/toonpostprocess.rs`: the ACES filmic curve fit by Krzysztof Narkowicz (https://knarkowicz.wordpress.com/2016/01/06/aces-filmic-tone-mapping-curve/): "Fitted curve's HLSL source code (free to use under public domain CC0 or MIT license)". Used under CC0.
- The environment BRDF fit of `src/graphics3d/humanskinrenderer.rs` and the shader hash above are written for this engine. (The split sum integral is fitted by the engine's own least squares, not taken from another fit)

## Crates Requiring No Notice

These crates are used under a public domain dedication or a license without a notice condition. They are listed for reference only.

- adler2 2.0.1: `0BSD OR MIT OR Apache-2.0`, used under `0BSD`
- byteorder 1.5.0: `Unlicense OR MIT`, used under `Unlicense`
- byteorder-lite 0.1.0: `Unlicense OR MIT`, used under `Unlicense`
- memchr 2.8.3: `Unlicense OR MIT`, used under `Unlicense`
- to_method 1.1.0: `CC0-1.0`
